# Braid Ideal Work Log

This file is the chronological work log for the `braid-ideal` priority area. Use it for dated agent status, proof-attempt notes, checker narratives, handoffs, failed paths, and operator/developer communication that must remain discoverable but should not crowd the live priority tracker.

Use `brainstorming.md` for provisional ideas, insights, conceptual maps, and draft corpus-promotable text when this priority area has one. Use the main priority tracker in this directory for the compact current queue, blockers, promotion routing, and next action. Keep focused proof packets, certificates, app specs, and requirement notes in their own sibling files when they need a stable structure.

## Log Entries

### 2026-07-14 - Antipodal Binary Sub-Field Phase-Collapse Test Rejects a Prehistory-Independent Spiral

Executed the revised [antipodal binary dispatch](antipodal-binary-spiral-law-dispatch-packet.md) on the sub-field family before any rail crossing. Four admissible histories — circular, log-spiral-in, log-spiral-out, and smooth perturbed — were matched at the same $(R_0,s_0)$ within each $s_0\in\{0.25,0.5,0.75\}$ row and evolved to at least two memory depths with the corrected effective coupling $K_\star=0.90036722258974714607$. All twelve production rows remained below $c_f$ and completed with zero rejected steps.

Result: the post-memory $s(R)$ curves do not collapse. The fixed-radius speed spread is about $0.138$ across the $s_0=0.25$ common branch, grows $0.0477\to0.0536$ for $s_0=0.5$, and grows $0.0289\to0.0311$ for $s_0=0.75$. Synchronized seed spread also survives past $h$. Step-size, prehistory-segment, history-depth, and live token-gate parity refinements preserve the result. The durable [evidence packet](evidence/antipodal-binary-phase-collapse-2026-07-14.md), phase CSVs, and temporal-spread CSVs record the measurement.

Disposition `sub_field_phase_curves_do_not_collapse_seed_indexed_family`: no prehistory-independent spiral law $s(R)$ is measured for the antipodal binary, no exponent is fitted, and the negative is priority-only with `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`. The rail-caustic and token-dominance/MPFR discrimination test is explicitly deferred as a separate campaign.

- 2026-07-11: The operator accepted option (a) for `intrinsic_drift_precession_synthesis_decision`. The [scope decision](intrinsic-drift-precession-spin-interleaving-scope-decision.md) separates relative phase-and-tilt dynamics from ordered-frame spinor return, assigns distinct proof objects and falsifiers, and permits a future join only through a same-record invariant that controls both bounded dynamics and nontrivial $4\pi$ return. The discussion task was replaced by two priority-only programs; no implementation or corpus promotion was authorized. `DF-03` was removed from the Operator Discussion Queue and the remaining rows were reranked.

### 2026-07-07 - Sustained-Alignment Window Measured: Cross-Hit Clicks Are Transient and Insufficient; Both Surviving Retention Routes Reduce to One Formation-History Burden

Discharged the "exhibit or exclude sustained cross-hit alignment" fork left open by [chart spec Section 7](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#7-non-coincident-hinge-geometry-2026-07-07). The non-coincident cross-hit fold restores the Section 2 finite $A_2$ impulse only if the alignment $\mathbf v_j\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}_{ij}=c_f$ is *sustained* over a click window; Section 2 assumed the window depth $\mu_0$ by hand. New sibling script [fold-crossing-sustained-alignment-window-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-sustained-alignment-window-diagnostic.mjs) (`fold_crossing_sustained_alignment_window_diagnostic.v0`), tests [braid-ideal-fold-crossing-sustained-alignment-window-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-sustained-alignment-window-diagnostic.test.js) (5 passing); derivation in the new [Fold-Crossing Sustained-Alignment Window Packet](fold-crossing-sustained-alignment-window-packet.md).

Construction: a middle-binary source orbits radius $\rho_M$ at super-field $\beta_M$; an outer receiver orbits $\rho_R$ at $\beta_R$ (nested-shell middle/outer coplanar geometry). For each reception time over one source period the diagnostic solves the causal roots of the receiver against the source path, tracks the near-fold root ($\min|D_s|$), and measures the reception window over which the fold-born root persists at finite chord — deriving $\mu_0$, the recurrence $N_{\mathrm{click}}$, the booked Section 2 impulse, the receiver-normal sign, and the absorbed fraction of the certified per-rotation pump.

Result (moves proof state). The cross-hit fold is reached at finite chord in every configuration ($\min|D_s|$ to $0.003$, chord $O(\rho)$), confirming Section 7 — but the alignment is **transient, not sustained**: the fold is visited for only $\sim1$–$3\%$ of the source period, so with $N_{\mathrm{click}}=1$ the kinematic clicks book $<0.5\%$ of the pump (absorbed $0.1$–$0.4\%$ at $\beta_M\in\{1.05,1.1,1.2\}$). Extending the widest window to full dwell at fixed curvature (a conservative lower bound on the held-hinge transfer) raises the absorbed fraction only to $\approx2.3\%$ — still insufficient; the true held limit drives the fold non-transversal ($a\to0$), where the Section 2 formula no longer applies and the transacted action is set by the dwell dynamics.

Disposition `cross_hit_alignment_transient_not_sustained_insufficient_reduces_to_declared_hinge_branch`: sustained cross-hit alignment is not kinematically generic; a chart-clean pump-beating hinge click requires the declared middle-binary hinge branch that holds $v_M=c_f$ co-linearly over a dwell arc. That is exactly the dynamic-alignment / formation-history burden already isolated for induced sea orientational polarization, so **the two remaining retention routes reduce to one problem**: whether the braid's formation/recycling dynamics sustain a field-speed co-linear middle-binary hinge (equivalently, aligned sea orientational order) over a click window — a retained-history dynamic-braid computation, not another fixed-geometry or kinematic toy. Claim level: derivation for the transient-window measurement and the conservative full-dwell shortfall; assessment for the reduction to the formation-history burden. `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, `free_amplitude_parameter_count=0`, fail-closed at the seed-path certificate and `central_solver_retained_history_row`; no new schema or validator.

Coordination note: this builds on (does not duplicate) the parallel-thread [fold-crossing-hinge-geometry-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-hinge-geometry-diagnostic.mjs) / Section 7, which established the instantaneous cross-hit fold; this diagnostic adds the derived-window / absorber-inequality half.

Validation: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-sustained-alignment-window-diagnostic.test.js` (5 tests) and `node --check` pass.

Next closure goal: on the retained-history dynamic-braid row (once the seed-path certificate unblocks it), decide the shared dynamic-alignment / formation-history burden — does formation/ring-down hold the middle-binary field-speed co-linear hinge (or the aligned sea orientation) over a click window — which gates both surviving retention routes.

### 2026-07-07 - Independent Verification: Non-Coincident Cross-Hit Hinge Restores the A_2 Finite Impulse (Parallel-Thread Result Reproduced)

Independent reproduction of the non-coincident hinge geometry result landed the same day by the parallel-thread owner script [fold-crossing-hinge-geometry-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-hinge-geometry-diagnostic.mjs) ([spec Section 7](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#7-non-coincident-hinge-geometry-2026-07-07)). This thread built a separate hermetic diagnostic while the owner script was in flight; both reached the same conclusion, so the duplicate script/test/packet were removed and the finding is recorded here as verification rather than a second implementation.

Reproduced facts (independent hermetic model: source on a circle radius $\rho$ at rim fraction $\beta_s$, receiver at finite base separation, fold set $D_s=c_f-\mathbf V_s\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}=0$). (i) Mach threshold: the source causal fold exists only for $\beta_s>1$ (zero folds below field speed, two above; Mach half-angle $\arcsin(1/\beta_s)$), so the fold birth is the field-speed hinge itself. (ii) Finite-chord generic fold: for $\beta_s>1$ the two folds sit at finite chord $r_c=O(\rho)$ (e.g. $0.777$ at $\rho=1$, separation $1.5$, $\beta_s=1.1$), $D_s$ crosses zero linearly (curvature $a=0.775\neq0$ — generic Whitney $A_2$, not the symmetric cusp), $D_T$ finite. (iii) Coincidence control: shrinking the base separation reproduces the closed symmetric channel — chord collapses $0.777\to0.046$ and the (regularizer-free) Section-2 impulse diverges $2.66\to297$ as $r_c\to0$, matching the owner script's softening-independent-only-at-finite-chord finding. The parallel owner test suite passes independently here (`node --test tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-hinge-geometry-diagnostic.test.js`, 5 tests), and its disposition `non_coincident_cross_hit_hinge_restores_a2_finite_impulse_contingent_on_sustained_velocity_alignment` is confirmed.

Agreement on claim level and residual burden: the click-absorber route is not closed in general — the surviving requirement is a hinge that sustains $\mathbf v_j\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}_{ij}=c_f$ at finite separation (the same dynamic-alignment / formation-history burden gating induced sea orientational polarization), plus the impulse sign (polarity/geometry-dependent for a cross-hit) and the recurrence $N_{\mathrm{click}}$. Fail-closed throughout: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, blocked at the seed-path certificate and the central retained-history row; no duplicate artifact retained.

### 2026-07-07 - Dynamic Induced Polarization: First Absorptive Sea Tangential Channel, but Insufficient at Cluster Stiffness; All Fixed-Geometry / Single-Site Absorbers Now Closed

Answered the induced-sea-orientational-polarization half of the standing absorber closure goal at the level the static relaxation diagnostic could not reach. The existing [static induced-polarization diagnostic](sh-0-sea-diagnostic-candidate-model.md#induced-sea-orientational-polarization---2026-07-07) holds the central braid fixed and relaxes neighbor orientations to an energy equilibrium — a RADIAL retention question (result: anti-retentive). The closure goal names the dynamic TANGENTIAL response: neighbor orientations responding with a phase lag to the central braid's *rotating* wake. New sibling script [sh-0-sea-dynamic-polarization-drive-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/sh-0-sea-dynamic-polarization-drive-diagnostic.mjs) (`sh_0_sea_dynamic_polarization_drive_diagnostic.v0`), tests [braid-ideal-sh-0-sea-dynamic-polarization-drive-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-sh-0-sea-dynamic-polarization-drive-diagnostic.test.js) (3 passing); derivation in the new [Dynamic Induced Polarization section](sh-0-sea-diagnostic-candidate-model.md#dynamic-induced-polarization---tangential-absorber-test---2026-07-07).

Results (moves proof state). (i) Threefold-drive lemma (derivation, witnessed): the $C_3$-symmetric rotating braid presents a $2\pi/3$-periodic wake at each fixed neighbor, so the orientational drive torque has only threefold harmonics $m=3,6,9$ ($m=1,2,4,5$ vanish to $3\times10^{-15}$); the axial dipole $2(1,1,1)\parallel\hat{\mathbf n}$ is rotation-invariant and supplies no AC drive, so the leading drive is the $m=3$ harmonic ($\sum_k|\tau_k^{(3)}|^2$ from $0.109$ at $\beta=0.1$ to $0.529$ at $\beta=0.9$, causal delay included). (ii) Sign-definite absorption (derivation): unlike the static sea's orientation torque $c_0$ (Corollary S: exact zero cyclic work), a phase-lagged response dissipates $\langle P\rangle=\tfrac12\sum_m(m\omega)\chi''(m\omega)\sum_k|\tau_k^{(m)}|^2\ge0$ — the first sea tangential channel that always drains (never pumps) the rotation, with effective damping $\Phi_{\mathrm{tan}}^{\mathrm{ind}}\approx(0.03$–$0.17)\chi''(3\omega)$. (iii) Threshold (computation + assessment): absorbing the certified pump $c_1\beta$ needs $\chi''(3\omega)\gtrsim8.6$–$19.2$, i.e. orientational stiffness $K\lesssim0.026$–$0.058$; the cluster stiffness the static relaxation exhibits ($K\sim0.3$–$0.5$, $\chi''_{\max}\sim1$–$1.7$) falls short by $5$–$15\times$, so at plausible $\chi''\sim1$ the damping is $\le10\%$ of the pump — the same order as the static sea and breathing channels.

Disposition `dynamic_induced_polarization_absorptive_but_insufficient_at_cluster_stiffness`: the channel is genuinely dissipative but insufficient under the cluster-derived stiffness; sufficiency requires a near-Goldstone-soft neighbor orientation mode ($K\lesssim0.05$) *and* resonance tuning ($\gamma\approx K/3\omega$), reducing the branch to one internal quantity — the neighbor braid orientational AC susceptibility $\chi''(3\omega)$ — that only a retained-history dynamic-braid computation can fix. Claim level: derivation for the threefold-drive lemma, absorptive sign, and damping functional form; computation for the drive harmonics and $\chi''$ threshold; assessment for the cluster-stiffness shortfall. `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, fail-closed at the seed-path certificate; the reported $\chi''$ is a threshold, never a fitted amplitude (`free_amplitude_parameter_count=0`).

Closure-goal synthesis. With this result, every fixed-geometry and single-site-self-hit tangential absorber is now closed or insufficient: static aligned sea a certified non-absorber ($\le10\%$, Corollary S); common-phase breathing $\le27\%$; dynamic induced polarization $\le10\%$ at cluster stiffness (this entry); and the symmetric circular self-hit fold is a cusp born at coincidence with an absorptive branch-orientation sign but a regulator-dependent magnitude (no regularization-independent chart impulse — the fold-crossing chart-spec Section 6 measurement and the click-impulse diagnostic). The regularization-independent hinge-click impulse the closure goal named is therefore falsified on the realized symmetric channel; the two surviving retention routes are a **non-coincident hinge geometry** (asymmetric or nested-shell self-hit/cross-hit born at finite chord $r_c=O(\rho)$, restoring the $A_2$ finite impulse) and a **near-Goldstone-soft neighbor orientation mode** ($K\lesssim0.05$). Both require objects beyond the fixed-source / symmetric-channel toys.

Validation: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-sh-0-sea-dynamic-polarization-drive-diagnostic.test.js` (3 tests) and `node --check` pass.

Next closure goal: exhibit or exclude a non-coincident hinge geometry (asymmetric/nested-shell configuration whose self-hit or cross-hit fold is born at finite chord), and compute the neighbor braid orientational AC susceptibility on a retained-history dynamic-braid row to decide whether $\chi''(3\omega)$ reaches the derived absorber threshold.

### 2026-07-07 - Fold-Crossing Branch-Orientation Sign Verified Absorptive: Refines the Chart-Spec Rigid-Rotation m=1

Verified the magnitude half and refined the branch-orientation half of the same-day [Fold-Crossing Chart Spec](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md) for `self_hit_held_release_solver_row` (Group A item 3). Before writing, inspected `git status --short --untracked-files=all` and the priority tracker; the chart-spec / fixture / measurement artifacts had landed, so this thread verifies rather than duplicates. New sibling files: verification packet [fold-crossing-click-impulse-packet.md](fold-crossing-click-impulse-packet.md), owner script [fold-crossing-click-impulse-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-click-impulse-diagnostic.mjs) (`fold_crossing_click_impulse_diagnostic.v0`), and tests [braid-ideal-fold-crossing-click-impulse-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-click-impulse-diagnostic.test.js) (9 passing). The diagnostic drives [held-release-causal-wake-toy.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/held-release-causal-wake-toy.mjs) via CLI for the real worldlines and reproduces the naive regularization sweep; a hermetic canonical rotating-channel model with the same fold sign structure backs the unit tests.

Construction: on the incoming worldline of one receiver site through the field-speed hinge, the same-source causal residual $F_T(s)=\lVert\mathbf X(T)-\mathbf X(s)\rVert-c_f(T-s)$ is solved for its grazing root, and two tangential impulses are integrated side by side — the naive absolute branch weight $\lvert D_T/D_s\rvert$ with the toy's Jacobian-floor clamp, softened denominator, and self-hit-minimum-delay gate, and the chart-defined signed orientation $m=D_T/D_s$ with exact $D_s$ and none of those regularizers. Witnesses: nstep convergence at fixed cut, regularization invariance across softening / floor / min-delay, and a coincidence cut-distance sensitivity sweep.

Result (moves proof state): the chart spec's coincidence birth is confirmed directly — the executable cut sweep gives $\mathcal I_{\mathrm{tan}}=-9.0,-23.1,-55.7,-137.1,-588.3$ as the resolved chord runs $0.04\to0.0025$, so there is no regulator-independent magnitude. But the chart spec's $m=1$ is an artifact of its fixed-$\beta$ rigid-rotation reconstruction, which sets $\mathbf V(T)=\mathbf V(s_r)$ and freezes the tangential speed the pump is changing. On the actual accelerating crossing the receiver crosses field speed ahead of its own emitting past, so $D_T<0<D_s$ and $m<0$ at every reception time past the hinge, arbitrarily close to birth: measured on `vt095`, $D_s=+0.00629$, $D_T=-0.00270$, $m=-0.43$ at $T=0.4304$ (hinge $0.42893$), steepening to $m=-0.87$ at $T=0.44$; `vt080` and `vt099` carry the identical absorptive sign. The signed impulse is bit-identical across the softening / Jacobian-floor / self-hit-minimum-delay triples that drive the recorded naive $12.4\to9.8\to10.6\,c_f$ ejection drift (reproduced: `vt095` final field-speed ratio $12.39$ / $9.83$ / $10.55$ under floor $0.05$-mindelay $0.002$ / floor $0.2$ / mindelay $0.05$), and its sign is absorptive at every coincidence cut.

Disposition `absorptive_branch_orientation_sign_regulator_independent_magnitude_coincidence_sensitive`. The symmetric-channel hinge click is not closed by an ejective sign, as the naive toy and a fixed-$\beta$ reading imply; its direction is absorptive and its amount is set by the coincidence-stratum spatial self-regularization the point-transceiver ontology already carries. This is a discrepancy against the chart-spec closure that the branch-orientation sign, not the magnitude, decides. Each witnessed crossing books a diagnostic-only $h_{\mathrm{act}}$ ledger row (transacted cycle increment $-1\cdot h_{\mathrm{act}}$, direction only, no action closure), reusing the closed-cycle action-unit convention with no new validator or schema.

Checker status: `node --check` on the new script passed; `node --test tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-click-impulse-diagnostic.test.js` passed (9 tests); `node scripts/check-polarity-notation-drift.mjs` and `node scripts/validate-content.mjs --check --strict` passed. Claim level: diagnostic/verification rows only; `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`; every output fail-closed at the seed-path certificate and `central_solver_retained_history_row`; no retained branch, no accepted evidence, no stability claim.

Open operator decision: admit the sign-definite, spatially-self-regularized symmetric-channel hinge transfer for the absorber program, or require a non-coincident hinge geometry (chart-spec route) for a chart-clean magnitude. Named follow-up producer boundary: a `central_solver_retained_history_row` contract carrying the signed branch orientation across $\Sigma$ (not $\lvert m\rvert$), the coincidence-stratum spatial self-regularization, and same-record $h_{\mathrm{act}}$ ledger rows.

Next closure goal: operator adjudication of the absorptive-sign versus chart-clean-magnitude question, then either the signed-orientation central-solver contract extension or the non-coincident hinge-geometry hunt.

### 2026-07-07 - Induced Sea Orientational Polarization Falsified: Overdamped Relaxation Drives the Sea Anti-Retentive, Aligned-Order Caveat Hardens

Closed the last standing static/quasi-static sea-alignment route from the orientational-order analysis. New sibling script [sh-0-sea-induced-polarization-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/sh-0-sea-induced-polarization-diagnostic.mjs) (`sh_0_sea_induced_polarization_diagnostic.v0`) with tests in [braid-ideal-sh-0-sea-induced-polarization-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-sh-0-sea-induced-polarization-diagnostic.test.js) (6 tests passing); recorded run `--samples=6 --iteration-cap=2000 --convergence-tolerance=1e-7`. Reuses exports from [sh-0-sea-diagnostic-candidate-model.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/sh-0-sea-diagnostic-candidate-model.mjs) (kernel, FCC sites/directions, floor, fail-closed authorization).

Construction: each of the 12 FCC nearest-neighbor braids carries one declared $SO(3)$ orientation on the held six-site face-opposite decoration (dipole reversal reachable within $SO(3)$, so no separate conjugation variable), evolving by overdamped gradient relaxation on the total potential-superposition energy of the 13-braid cluster — central-shell plus shell-shell bonds, central braid held fixed as the `SH-0` target identity, torque $\tau_k=\sum_{i\in k}(x_i-X_k)\times F_i$, $F_i=-\nabla_i U$, kernel constants identical to the wake-sum runs. Relaxation step, iteration cap, and convergence tolerance are declared integration parameters (not response amplitudes): `free_amplitude_parameter_count=0` at run, spacing, and ensemble levels. Order parameter is the mean per-neighbor dipole projection onto the central dipole; $\Pi_R\mathcal A^{\mathrm{sea}}$ of the relaxed shell is tested against the escape floor $-0.0934863484737535$ with the standard deadband.

Result (moves proof state on the failure condition — a clean falsification): relaxing from the aligned start, the paired-antiphase start, and every seeded disordered start, the relaxed radial response is outward ($\Pi_R>0$) at every declared spacing $a_{\mathrm{FCC}}\in\{3,3.5,4,4.25,4.5,5,5.25\}$, reversing the sign of the assumed-aligned inward reference and clustering near the anti-retentive conjugate limit. At the named candidate spacing $4.25$: relaxed aligned $\Pi_R=+0.2837$, disordered mean $\Pi_R=+0.2934$ (min $+0.2511$, max $+0.3365$, $0/6$ inward crossings), against the assumed-aligned reference $-0.2833$. No start crosses the inward floor at any spacing; the only inward disordered seed is one of six near-overlap samples at $a_{\mathrm{FCC}}=3.0$ against an outward mean $+0.587$. Exact controls verified as designed: conjugation antisymmetry (all-neighbor conjugation flips $\Pi_R$ to $\le3\times10^{-16}$), paired-antiphase null in both the charge-conjugation and $SO(3)$ $180^\circ$-rotation representations, and paired-antiphase as an exact response null but not an energy fixed point (torque $\approx1.47$ at $4.25$), so relaxation escapes it. The tension the run was built to test resolves rather than being assumed away: the center-shell energy prefers dipole-reversed neighbors ($+0.864$ aligned versus $-0.864$ conjugate at $4.25$), and overdamped relaxation realizes that preference in response space; neighbor-neighbor frustration keeps the dipole order parameter small and mildly positive ($O\approx0.12$-$0.23$) because the FCC dipole-shell sum cancels, but the retention-relevant higher-multipole response is robustly anti-retentive.

Disposition `induced_polarization_relaxes_anti_retentive_no_bounded_window`. The static induced-polarization retention route is falsified at the diagnostic level: left to relax under its own potential-superposition energetics, the fixed-geometry sea polarizes to expel the central braid. Combined with the frustration, paired-antiphase null, and stochastic-disorder rejections, no static or quasi-static mechanism in the fixed-geometry sea supplies the central-aligned order that guaranteed retention requires. The aligned-order caveat on every sea-screened held-release row therefore hardens from "conditional on an alignment mechanism" to an open formation-history / dynamic-alignment burden, and the mechanism hunt sharpens toward the hinge-click absorber route (`self_hit_held_release_solver_row`). Recorded in the [model packet](sh-0-sea-diagnostic-candidate-model.md#induced-sea-orientational-polarization---2026-07-07) and the [run matrix](sh-run-matrix.md). Claim level: diagnostic rows only; `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, every output fail-closed at the central seed-path certificate; no retained branch, no accepted evidence, no stability claim.

Next action: `self_hit_held_release_solver_row` (hinge-click absorber route) is now the sole remaining retention-mechanism candidate at the sea/environment level; the producer boundary is the regularization-independent fold-crossing treatment on the central-solver retained-history path.

### 2026-07-07 - Sea-Screened Clock Corollary (Corollary S): the Aligned FCC Sea is a Certified Non-Absorber of the Tangential Pump

Decided the sea-export branch of the (S)-persistence / anti-damping absorption question. Extended the planar rotating-channel screen with the computed `sh0sea` sea term and certified the sign of the net along-velocity force against the certified pump band. New section [Corollary S](delayed-escape-certificate-lemma-proof-packet.md#corollary-s---sea-screened-clock-the-aligned-fcc-sea-is-a-non-absorber) in the [Delayed Escape Certificate Lemma Proof Packet](delayed-escape-certificate-lemma-proof-packet.md); owner-script extension `seaEnclosure`/`certifySea` in [planar-tangential-screen-interval-certificate.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/planar-tangential-screen-interval-certificate.mjs), tests extended to 6 passing.

Construction and result (moves proof state on the success condition — a certified sign decision for the sea tangential term against the certified pump band): the attempt `aa` FCC nearest-neighbor shell (12 like braids held static, dipole-aligned, $a_{\mathrm{FCC}}=4.25$, zero fitted amplitude, same kernel row — $c_f=1$, $\kappa=1$, zero softening, receiver-normal over source-normal, partner-wake plus one-way static sea) is placed around the axis-neutral rotating braid. Because the sources are held static (source Jacobian $1$; delay does not move them), the sea along-velocity projection decomposes exactly as $\Pi_{\mathrm{tan}}^{\mathrm{sea}}(\beta,\varphi)=c_0(\varphi)-\beta Q(\varphi)$, both $\beta$-independent geometric sums over rotation phase $\varphi$. Two certified facts decide the absorber question: (i) $c_0$ (the static orientation-locking torque) is odd about the $C_3$-symmetric phases with exact zero rotation-cycle average (witness: cyclic mean $\le4\times10^{-17}$, oddness residual $\le3\times10^{-16}$) — it transacts no net tangential action; (ii) the velocity-linear coefficient is interval-certified $-0.1269\le Q(\varphi)\le0.2746$ over one $2\pi/3$ period (4096 phase boxes, outward-rounded, min sea distance $\ge1.469$), so $\sup_\varphi\lvert Q\rvert\le0.2746<c_1=2.881$ with non-absorber margin $\ge2.606$. The aligned FCC sea removes at most $\approx10\%$ (cyclic mean $\approx2.4\%$) of the certified pump and cannot cancel it. The octahedral chart reproduces the recorded release-instant $\Pi_R=-0.2833417889031177$ to 12 digits, so the sea screen and the recorded dipole wake sum are the same object.

Decision `sea_screened_clock_survives_aligned_sea_non_absorber`: the certified clock survives sea screening with the rate reduced only from $c_1$ to $c_1-\sup\lvert Q\rvert\ge2.606$, giving $\Delta t\le1.495\,c_f\rho^2/\kappa$ (isolated $1.353$; inside the Lemma T tube $\theta c_1-\sup\lvert Q\rvert\ge1.166>0$ at $\theta=\tfrac12$, so the sea-screened clock is unconditional there too). The certified inward radial projection $\Pi_R=-0.283$ holds the radius — inducing the recorded sub-field return turns — while the uncancelled pump drives the sites through $c_f$: radial support without a tangential absorber, exactly matching the recorded [sea-screened held-release rows](sh-0-sea-diagnostic-candidate-model.md#sea-screened-held-release-rows---2026-07-07). Order-robust: conjugate order flips $Q$ (same $\sup\lvert Q\rvert$), paired-antiphase gives $Q\equiv0$ and $\Pi_R\equiv0$, isotropic disorder averages to zero, so the non-absorption holds in every orientation order; the orientational-order caveat (aligned order is frustrated, not self-selected) conditions only the sea's radial retention supply, never the clock. Claim level: interval certificate conditional on the declared rounding model for the $Q$ bound; derivation for the decomposition, $c_0$ cancellation, order-robustness, and clock; no retained branch, no accepted evidence, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`. Consequence: the (S)-persistence burden's sea-export branch is discharged; the remaining absorber candidates narrow to hinge clicks (`self_hit_held_release_solver_row`) and induced sea orientational polarization.

Validation: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-planar-tangential-screen-interval-certificate.test.js` (6 tests) passes.

Next closure goal: the (S)-persistence lemma via the two remaining absorbers — the regularization-independent fold-crossing (hinge click) treatment on the central-solver retained-history path, and the induced sea orientational-polarization linear response the fixed-source diagnostic cannot compute.

### 2026-07-07 - Rigid-to-Released Transfer Lemma Proved: (S)-Failure Clock Unconditional Inside a Certified Tube; Rigid-Family Rejection Upgraded to an Open Neighborhood

Lemma T is a Lipschitz-in-history deviation bound: if a released row's per-site history stays within $(\delta,\nu)$ of a rigid planar-hexagon rotating history over one window depth ($3\rho/c_f$), then its along-velocity wake acceleration stays within $T=L_x(\beta)\delta+L_v(\beta)\nu$ of the certified screen value $\Phi_{\mathrm{tan}}(\beta)$. Proof chain: root shift $\le2\delta/(1-\beta)$ through the Lemma 5 transversality floor (T1); pair-vector displacement $D=2\delta/(1-\beta)$ with per-partner root-distance floors (T2); unit-vector, shifted-event velocity (ansatz acceleration $\beta^2$ exact), normal, and branch-weight perturbation steps (T3-T6); mean-value inverse-square bound with $\lVert D(\mathbf u/\lvert\mathbf u\rvert^3)\rVert=2/\lvert\mathbf u\rvert^3$ (T7); sum and velocity-direction projection (T8). Consequence: no relative equilibrium, periodic row, or slowly varying sub-field row exists inside the certified tube — the rotating-wave packet's rigid-family rejection upgrades from the exact ansatz to an open neighborhood, so admissible eigen-braids must sit beyond the tube in breathing amplitude, non-planarity, or history deviation, or be sea-coupled. Recorded constants: per-partner root-distance floors $(0.524,1.029,1.486,1.749,1.018)\rho$; tube $1.9\times10^{-3}\rho$ at $\beta=0.25$, $1.7\times10^{-3}\rho$ at $\beta=0.5$, $3.3\times10^{-4}\rho$ at $\beta=0.9$, minimum $4.8\times10^{-5}\rho$ near the field-speed edge; witness conservatism $340\times$-$1900\times$, so tube sharpening (direction-resolved constants) is the named follow-up. The seed-path authority must therefore be a distinct non-repo seed-path authority object/package using the seed-path-specific `accepted:held-release-seed-path-rows:`, `external-authority:held-release-seed-path-rows:`, `external-verification:held-release-seed-path-rows:`, and `external-authority-package:held-release-seed-path-rows:` families.

None can authorize the seed-path certificate.

Decision: this cycle sharpens the exact non-repo source authority but still produces no accepted certificate. ### 2026-07-07 - Fold-Crossing Chart Spec Draws the Self-Hit Producer Boundary

Drafted the producer-boundary specification for `self_hit_held_release_solver_row` (Group A item 3) as the packet-level definition of the controlled hinge click, consuming the same-day self-hit ejection witness as its failure-mode fixture. New packet `reference/priorities/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-chart-spec.md`; sibling fixture `scripts/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-chart-fixture.mjs` with tests `tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-chart-fixture.test.js` (8 tests, all passing). No native central-solver production code, ABI, held-release toy default, corpus chapter, or the cited escape-certificate packet was touched. Meta wrapper: `reference/op/codex-goal-seeking-prompt-template.md`.

Normal-form statement (derivation). On the same-source directed pair the causal residual $F_{ij}=r_{ij}-c_f(T-T_{\mathrm{em}})$ near the field-speed crossing takes the Whitney-fold ($A_2$) normal form $F_{ij}(\tau;T)=\tfrac12 a(\tau-\tau_*)^2-\mu(T)$ with fold curvature $a=\partial_\tau^2 F_{ij}|_*$ and unfolding parameter $\mu(T)=-F_{ij}(\tau_*;T)$. Chart coordinates $(\xi,\mu)$, $\xi=\tau-\tau_*$, with $\Sigma_{ij}=\{\mu=0\}$. The same-source root pair is $\xi_\pm=\pm\sqrt{2\mu/a}$ (born at $\mu=0$, absent for $\mu<0$), realizing the integer root-count change of the click. The source-normal denominator is $D_s^\pm=a\xi_\pm=\pm\sqrt{2a\mu}\to0$, so the toy branch weight $|m^\pm|=|D_T/D_s|=|D_T|/\sqrt{2a\mu}\sim\mu^{-1/2}$ diverges (recorded up to 163 at the 0.05 floor). Its replacement is the density-of-states reading of $D_s$ from `architrino.md`: $1/D_s$ is a root-map Jacobian to be integrated, not a coefficient to be evaluated.

Click-impulse bound (derivation for finiteness, hypothesis for coefficients). The control-sweep identity $\dot\mu=-\partial_T F_{ij}(\tau_*;T)=D_T$ makes the receiver-normal numerator the fold-crossing rate. With $dT=d\mu/D_T$ the per-branch reception-time impulse is $\int f^\pm\,dT=(\kappa/r_c^2)\int_0^{\mu_0}d\mu/\sqrt{2a\mu}=(\kappa/r_c^2)\sqrt{2\mu_0/a}$: $D_T$ cancels exactly and the $\mu^{-1/2}$ caustic is integrable, giving the finite chart impulse $|\Delta\mathbf p_{\mathrm{click}}|=\chi\,(\kappa/r_c^2)\sqrt{2\mu_0/a}+O(\mu_0^{3/2})$, independent of the Jacobian floor, softening, and time step (they only cap the pointwise peak, not the integral). On the rigid channel ($r_c\sim\rho$, $a\sim\beta^2 c_f^2/\rho$, $\mu_0\sim\rho$) this scales as $|\Delta\mathbf p_{\mathrm{click}}|\sim\kappa/(\beta c_f\rho)$. Absorber comparison against the certified pump ($\Phi_{\mathrm{tan}}\in[2.881,2.925]\beta$, clock $\Delta t\le1.353\,c_f\rho^2/\kappa$): one click per rotation absorbs the pump iff $\beta\le1/(2\pi c_1)\approx0.055$; near the field-speed ceiling it falls short by $\approx18\beta$, so sufficiency needs $\approx18$ clicks per rotation or a larger per-click $\chi\sqrt{\mu_0/a}$. Both $\kappa$ and $\rho$ cancel in the ratio — sufficiency is a pure rim-fraction threshold.

This is the success branch, not the failure branch: a finite regularization-independent impulse exists, so the hinge-click absorber route is not closed at the kernel level; the click-absorber question becomes a computable central-solver target with the declared same-record ledger contract (action-ledger $h_{\mathrm{act}}$ rows, root-ledger fold-transition rows via the existing retained/appeared/disappeared/folded classes, and receiver-normal fields carrying the density-of-states flag and the chart-integrated weight that replaces the pointwise $|m|$ on fold-flagged rows).

Fixture / tests run. `node --test tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-chart-fixture.test.js` — 8 pass / 0 fail. Asserts: finite chart impulse; cusp ($a\le0$) falls out of the chart; $D_T$-independence (spread 0); regularization-independence (spread below $10^{-12}$); the naive-ejection witness IS regularization-dependent ($v_{\max}$ spread $2.6\,c_f$); the executable rejection predicate returns false on the chart impulse and true on the witness; the $\beta\le0.055$ absorber threshold with $\approx18\times$ shortfall at the ceiling; fail-closed report fields.

Queue / packet disposition. `self_hit_held_release_solver_row` note updated with the spec/fixture and disposition `fold_crossing_chart_spec_finite_impulse_absorber_question_computable`; status stays `next` (spec is not implementation, fail-closed at `central_solver_retained_history_row`). Standing constraints honored: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema, signed polarity-unit notation, potential-superposition and causal-delay terminology throughout.

Blockers. None for the spec itself; the click-absorber decision is blocked on the central-solver retained-history implementation of the Section 3 contract (the geometric coefficients $\chi,\mu_0,a$ and the recurrence $N_{\mathrm{click}}$ can only be measured on the retained-history row).

Next closure goal: implement the fold-crossing chart contract on the central-solver retained-history path for `self_hit_held_release_solver_row` and measure $(\chi,\mu_0,a,N_{\mathrm{click}})$ on the `vt095` sea+self-hit row, testing $N_{\mathrm{click}}\Delta\beta_{\mathrm{click}}\ge\int_{\mathrm{rot}}d\beta_{\mathrm{pump}}$ against the certified pump.

### 2026-07-07 - Fold-Crossing Chart Measurement on vt095: Symmetric Self-Hit Is a Cusp at Coincidence

Implemented the fold-crossing chart contract as a measurement over the recorded `sh0sea-a4.25-selfhit-vt095-moving-prehistory` crossing and tested the Section 2 finite-impulse derivation of `fold-crossing-chart-spec.md`. New owner script `scripts/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-chart-measurement.mjs` with tests `tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-chart-measurement.test.js` (7 tests, all passing). No native central-solver production code or ABI, held-release toy default, corpus chapter, or cited packet was modified. The toy was run read-only (`--surface-speed-fraction 0.95 --prehistory-mode moving-prehistory --fcc-sea-spacing 4.25 --include-self-hits`) to extract the recorded crossing inputs.

Recorded crossing inputs: $\rho=0.816497$ (`referencePerpendicularRadius` $=\sqrt{2/3}$), $c_f=1$, $\kappa=1$, first field-speed crossing $T_c=0.43$ with `speedMax` $\beta_c=1.00196$, first self-hit root opens at $T_c$ exactly, `maxBranchWeight` $=163.5$, `smallJacobianRoots` $=24$, self-hit min delay $=dt=0.002$.

Measurement (derivation on the reconstructed rigid-rotation self-hit residual $F=2\rho\sin(\omega\Delta/2)-c_f\Delta$, $\omega=\beta c_f/\rho$; the near-constant crossing radius makes this a faithful model). Two symmetry-forced degeneracies coincide at the self-hit onset:

- Cusp, not fold. Over the offset window $\varepsilon_\beta=\beta-1$ the measurement finds $r_c^2/\varepsilon_\beta\to16.0$ and $a/\sqrt{\varepsilon_\beta}\to-1.50$ (both ratios constant to $<3\%$), so $r_c\sim\sqrt{\varepsilon_\beta}\to0$ and $a=\partial^2_\tau F\sim\sqrt{\varepsilon_\beta}\to0$: an $A_3$ cusp, not the generic $A_2$ Whitney fold Section 2 assumed. At $\beta_c=1.00196$: $\Delta_*=r_c=0.1768$, $a=-0.0666$, $D_s=D_T=0.00393$.
- Branch orientation unity. By reflection symmetry $D_T=D_s$ exactly, so $m=D_T/D_s=1$ throughout — no caustic in the orientation factor. The recorded `maxBranchWeight` $163$ is therefore a partner-pair or floor artifact, not the geometric self-root.
- Fold born at coincidence. $r_c=c_f\Delta_*\to0$ at birth, so the causal-root fold coincides with the spatial coincidence stratum $\{r_{ij}=0\}$ (the other singular locus in `architrino.md`, regulated by the spatial regulator, not by the fold chart). The self-hit branch force $f=\kappa|m|/(r_c^2+\mathrm{reg}^2)$ with $|m|=1$, $r_c^2\propto\varepsilon_\beta$ gives $\Delta\beta_{\mathrm{click}}$ that grows $0.30\to5.4$ as the spatial regulator runs $0.2\to0.001$ (log growth) — regulator-dependent, the mechanism of the toy's regularization-dependent ejection ($v_{\max}$ across $12.4/9.8/10.6\,c_f$).

Measured $(\chi,\mu_0,a,N_{\mathrm{click}})$ outcome: $a=-0.0666$ (and $\to0$ at the crossing), $r_c=0.1768$ (and $\to0$), $\chi$ and $\mu_0$ undefined because the $A_2$ chart is degenerate (cusp at coincidence), $N_{\mathrm{click}}=1$ per crossing (the opened self-hit branch is continuous, not a recurring discrete click). Absorber test $N_{\mathrm{click}}\Delta\beta_{\mathrm{click}}\ge\int_{\mathrm{rot}}d\beta_{\mathrm{pump}}$: even at the regulator-inflated value $4.31$ against pump-per-rotation $2\pi c_1\kappa/(c_f^2\rho)=22.17$, absorbed fraction $\approx0.19$, and not a legitimate chart impulse.

Disposition `symmetric_self_hit_fold_is_cusp_at_coincidence_no_regulator_independent_click_impulse` — the failure branch for the symmetric circular self-hit channel (the channel the held-release toy and `vt095` actually realize). No finite chart-defined click impulse exists there because the fold births at coincidence, so the naive hinge-click absorber route is closed at the kernel level for this channel. This does not close the general click hypothesis: Section 2's finite impulse survives for any hinge geometry realizing a non-coincident generic fold ($r_c=O(\rho)$ at birth), which the symmetric single-site self-hit cannot. The spec was corrected accordingly (new Section 6; Section 5 disposition marked superseded for this channel). Combined with the same-day Corollary S (aligned FCC sea a certified non-absorber, item 1) and the induced-polarization negative, both named kernel-level absorbers are now closed on the symmetric channel.

Fixture/tests run. `node --test tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-chart-measurement.test.js` — 7 pass / 0 fail (self-hit root exists only above $c_f$; $m=1$ reflection symmetry; cusp-at-coincidence confirmed; click impulse regulator-dependent; absorber verdict one click $\approx0.19$; contract rows carry the degeneracy flags; fail-closed report). The prior `tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-chart-fixture.test.js` (8 tests) still passes.

Queue / packet disposition. `self_hit_held_release_solver_row` note extended with the measurement and verdict; status stays `next` (measurement is not implementation, fail-closed at `central_solver_retained_history_row`). Standing constraints honored: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema, signed polarity-unit notation, potential-superposition and causal-delay terminology.

Blockers. The symmetric-channel hinge-click route is closed; the surviving possibility requires exhibiting a non-coincident hinge geometry, which needs an asymmetric or nested-shell self-hit/cross-hit configuration not yet available in the toy.

Next closure goal: test whether a non-coincident hinge geometry exists — an asymmetric or nested-shell configuration whose hinge fold is born at finite chord $r_c=O(\rho)$, restoring the Section 2 $A_2$ finite impulse — or else accept the symmetric-channel closure and route the retention program to induced sea orientational polarization (dynamic-alignment / formation-history burden).

### 2026-07-07 - Non-Coincident Hinge Geometry: Self-Hit Coincidence Theorem, Cross-Hit Finite-Chord Rescue

Resolved the geometry fork left by the vt095 measurement. New owner script `scripts/braid-ideal/fold-crossing-hinge-geometry-diagnostic.mjs` with tests `tests/braid-ideal-fold-crossing-hinge-geometry-diagnostic.test.js` (5 tests, all passing). No native central-solver code/ABI, toy default, corpus chapter, or cited packet modified. Spec `fold-crossing-chart-spec.md` extended (new Section 7; Section 6 branch-orientation bullet reconciled with the parallel signed-orientation packet).

Two results:

- Self-hit coincidence theorem (derivation). For any smooth worldline a same-source root nucleates from $\Delta\to0$ exactly at the speed crossing, because $\lvert\mathbf X(T)-\mathbf X(T-\Delta)\rvert=\lvert\mathbf v\rvert\Delta+O(\Delta^2)$ forces the root condition $\lvert\mathbf v\rvert\Delta=c_f\Delta$ to birth at $\Delta=0$. Confirmed on an explicitly asymmetric path (radial drift $0.15$ + tangential pump $0.4$): fold chord $r_c=0.073/0.178/0.345/0.647$ at $\beta=1.02/1.05/1.1/1.2$, $\to0$ as $\beta\to1$. This upgrades the vt095 circular measurement to a general statement — no single-site self-hit path escapes coincidence birth, so the self-hit magnitude is never chart-clean.
- Cross-hit finite-chord fold (derivation + measurement). A fold between distinct sites $i\neq j$ sits where $D_{s,ij}=c_f-\mathbf v_j\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}_{ij}=0$ (the alignment condition: source moving along the sightline to the receiver's causal position at $c_f$). Head-on realization: $r_c=L=1.0$ (finite), $a=\partial^2_\tau F=-g=-0.5\neq0$ (generic $A_2$), Section-2 impulse $=1.41$ finite, and softening-independent (impulse flat to $3\times10^{-4}$ below softening $0.02$) because the finite chord bounds the kernel $1/(r_c^2+\varepsilon^2)$ away from the coincidence stratum. The residual $|m|$ caustic is the ordinary $A_2$ density-of-states factor (spec Section 1.3), regulated by the causal-root Jacobian, not by softening; decoupling holds while the click window stays non-coincident ($L$ larger than the window reach — a real constraint, test-pinned).

Reconciliation with the parallel Fold-Crossing Click-Impulse Packet (`fold-crossing-click-impulse-diagnostic.mjs`, another thread): it corroborates the coincidence birth on the recorded worldline and adds the signed-orientation result — on the accelerating crossing the receiver outruns its own past, so $D_T<0<D_s$ and $m<0$ (absorptive), and the naive toy ejection was the absolute-value weight $|m|$ discarding that sign. My Section 6 $m=1$ was the fixed-$\beta$ reconstruction artifact; corrected in the spec with a forward pointer. Combined picture: the symmetric self-hit transfer is directionally absorptive with a coincidence-sensitive (spatially-self-regularized) magnitude; a non-coincident cross-hit hinge additionally gives a chart-clean (softening-independent) magnitude, with sign set per geometry by $\operatorname{sign}(D_T)$.

### 2026-07-07 - Self-Hit Brake Measured on the Production Central-Solver Path: Sign Absorptive, Magnitude Reduces to a Declared Coincidence Stratum

Executed the central-solver branch of the self-hit hinge-absorber operator decision: rather than leave the signed-orientation contract as a named follow-up, drove it directly against the production causal-root runtime. Production reproduction (exact). On the recorded `vt095` crossing ($\rho=\sqrt{2/3}$, $\beta_c=1.00196$) the production same-source root returns chord radius $r_c=0.17662$, $D_s=D_T=0.0039177$, and emitted signed branch orientation `receiverNormalFactor` $=+1.0000$ — reproducing the rigid-rotation reconstruction to the digit and confirming the runtime already emits the signed $m=D_T/D_s$.

Three producer gaps localized on the production path (each a concrete, named line of the runtime, not a vague obligation): (1) `buildMovingCircularSameSourceRoot` exposes `branchWeight = unsignedReceiverNormalFactor` $=\lvert m\rvert$, discarding the emitted sign — exactly the naive-kernel error; (2) the same-source history is a fixed-$\omega$ rigid circle, so $D_T=D_s$, $m=+1$ identically, and it cannot carry the pump-driven tangential acceleration — reconstructing an accelerating crossing with the production receiver-normal definition returns $D_T<0<D_s$, $m<0$ at every reception time past the hinge; (3) coincidence handling is a numerical floor `safeDistance = max(EPSILON, distance)`, `EPSILON=1e-9`, so the signed click impulse is log-divergent as the declared stratum shrinks. The T3 engine already models this input as `jacobianFloorOrDeclaredStratum` (`missing_same_record_jacobian_floor_or_declared_stratum`).

## 2026-07-08 - Operator d0 decision applied to the symmetric self-hit brake (item 3)

Highest-priority Group A item `self_hit_held_release_solver_row` (item 3): the 2026-07-07 central-solver measurement had localized the symmetric self-hit brake to a single open producer gap - the declared coincidence-stratum length scale (`jacobianFloorOrDeclaredStratum`), an operator ontology decision the agent does not invent. The operator supplied it: the stratum is `d0`, of order the near-field two-body scale $\kappa\epsilon^2/c_f^2$ ($R_*$; in the minimum-circular-binary reading $d_0=R_{\mathrm{MCB}}$, the collapse-arresting radius; exact derivation from $(\kappa,\epsilon,c_f)$ open, [particle-masses](../../../content/markdown/aaa/assemblies/particle-masses.md)).

Evaluated at $d_0$ the symmetric single-site self-hit magnitude is $\approx50\times$ the certified per-rotation tangential pump, so the symmetric self-hit is dispositioned NOT load-bearing as a retention absorber. The decision was landed in both scripts so they tell one story:

- Source measurement [self-hit-brake-central-measurement.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/self-hit-brake-central-measurement.mjs): the `coincidence_stratum_is_a_numerical_floor` gap status flips to `resolved_by_operator_declared_d0_2026_07_08`; a new `operator_ontology_input` block records $d_0$; `declared_stratum_supplied_by_operator:true`.

## 2026-07-08 - Cross-hit hinge click-rate: the internal-frequency requirement (item 11)

Operator selected the non-coincident cross-hit hinge (over the soft neighbor-orientation mode) as the surviving tangential-pump absorber channel to derive-or-falsify. The spec Section 2.5 had reduced sufficiency to the per-rotation click count $N_{\mathrm{click}}$ and left it as "a hypothesis only the retained-history row can supply." That was too pessimistic: the click rate is a property of the frequency content of the configuration, not of the force ledger, so it admits a kinematic bound with no retained-history dynamics.

New owner script [cross-hit-hinge-click-rate-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/cross-hit-hinge-click-rate-diagnostic.mjs) (tests [braid-ideal-cross-hit-hinge-click-rate-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-cross-hit-hinge-click-rate-diagnostic.test.js), 5 passing) and [spec Section 8](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#8-cross-hit-hinge-click-rate-the-internal-frequency-requirement-2026-07-08):

- Result 1 (single-frequency braid -> $N_{\mathrm{click}}=0$): a cross-hit click is born where $A_{ij}=\mathbf v_j\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}_{ij}$ crosses $c_f$. If every site shares one $\omega$ the configuration is a global rigid rotation $\mathrm{Rot}(\omega t)$, which preserves inner products, so $A_{ij}(t)=\mathbf v_j(0)\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}_{ij}(0)=$ const. No sign change, no clicks; the exception $A_{ij}\equiv c_f$ is a static sustained hinge, not a recurring click. Delay-independent (rigid rotation carries causal roots to causal roots). Confirmed numerically: $A_{ij}$ constant to $1.6\times10^{-15}$ (single shell) / $2.7\times10^{-15}$ (multi-shell one frequency), zero crossings.
- Result 2 (needs $\ge2$ frequencies): a cross pair with frequencies $\omega_{\mathrm{out}},\omega_{\mathrm{in}}$ has $A_{ij}$ periodic in the beat $(\omega_{\mathrm{out}}-\omega_{\mathrm{in}})t$; over one outer rotation $N_{\mathrm{click}}\lesssim2P\lvert1-\omega_{\mathrm{in}}/\omega_{\mathrm{out}}\rvert$ across $P$ straddling directed pairs. Verified: ratio 1.5 -> 0 clicks (amplitude did not straddle $c_f$), ratio 6 -> 90 clicks tracking 5 beats/rotation.
- Verdict vs certified pump ($N_{\mathrm{click}}^{\mathrm{req}}=2\pi c_1\beta\approx18\beta$; pump-per-rotation reproduced at 22.17, per-click 1.25): single-frequency `closed_negative`; multi-frequency open only with a fast inner shell $\lvert1-\omega_{\mathrm{in}}/\omega_{\mathrm{out}}\rvert\gtrsim\pi c_1\beta/P$ (ratio $\gtrsim2.5$ for $P=6$, $\beta\to1$) AND amplitude straddling. Disposition `cross_hit_click_train_requires_fast_inner_shell_single_frequency_braid_gives_zero`.

This sharpens the Section 7 sustained-alignment/formation-history burden into two separable checkable conditions on the nested-shell architecture (fast inner frequency + amplitude straddling) and closes the single-shell reading of the cross-hit route negatively without the gated retained-history certificate chain. Fail-closed throughout: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema. Next closure goal: test whether the fast-inner-shell ratio clearing $N_{\mathrm{click}}\gtrsim18\beta$ is simultaneously compatible with amplitude straddling at an admissible inner rim fraction, or whether the two necessary conditions are mutually exclusive and close the cross-hit route as well.

Verification: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-cross-hit-hinge-click-rate-diagnostic.test.js` -> 5 pass, 0 fail.


## 2026-07-08 - Two-frequency coexistence test: the cross-hit conditions coexist (item 11)

Executing the prior closure goal. Section 8 left the cross-hit route open under two separable necessary conditions: (A) $N_{\mathrm{click}}\ge2\pi c_1\beta_{\mathrm{out}}$ (fast inner shell) and (B) amplitude straddling ($A_{ij}$ reaching $c_f$). Question: coexist at admissible $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}$, or mutually exclusive?

New owner script [cross-hit-hinge-coexistence-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/cross-hit-hinge-coexistence-diagnostic.mjs) (tests [braid-ideal-cross-hit-hinge-coexistence-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-cross-hit-hinge-coexistence-diagnostic.test.js), 5 passing) and [spec Section 9](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#9-two-frequency-coexistence-test-the-conditions-are-not-mutually-exclusive-2026-07-08). Two-shell config: 3 outer ($R_{\mathrm{out}}$, $\omega_{\mathrm{out}}$) + 3 inner ($R_{\mathrm{in}}=qR_{\mathrm{out}}$, $\omega_{\mathrm{in}}=r\omega_{\mathrm{out}}$), $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}=r\beta_{\mathrm{out}}q$; clicks counted on outer-receiver/inner-source directed pairs over one outer rotation.

Verdict: NOT mutually exclusive — they coexist. Reference point $r=2.5$, $q=0.44$, $\beta_{\mathrm{out}}=0.98$: $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}=1.08$, straddles ($\max A=1.08$), $N_{\mathrm{click}}=27\ge17.7$; coexistence region broad (20/36 grid points at $\beta_{\mathrm{out}}=0.98$; all $q\in[0.2,0.8]$ clear), minimum admissible $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}\approx1.18$. Structural facts:

- $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}\ge1$ is the single binding gate: sub-field inner shell (e.g. $r=2,q=0.44\Rightarrow\beta_{\mathrm{in}}=0.86$) gives exactly zero clicks; the train switches on as $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}$ crosses 1.
- Condition (A) delivers condition (B): $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}=r\beta_{\mathrm{out}}q$, so the frequency increase raising the click count also carries the inner binary super-field. Cooperative, not antagonistic.
- Frequency-ratio saturation: once $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}\ge1$, total absorption is $r$-independent (flat within 20% over $r\in[3,8]$) because click count $\propto r$ while per-click impulse falls as source centripetal accel grows. Control variables are $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}, q$, not raw $\omega_{\mathrm{in}}$.

Coefficient caveat: the indicative impulse total ($\approx3\times$ pump at reference) uses schematic Section 2.2 $\chi,\mu_0$, so its absolute size is order-of-magnitude only; robust results are coexistence, the $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}\ge1$ gate, and saturation. Disposition `cross_hit_absorber_conditions_coexist_binding_gate_is_super_field_inner_binary`. The cross-hit click absorber survives; the retention question reduces to one sharper burden: is a super-field inner binary ($\beta_{\mathrm{in}}\gtrsim1$) with coplanar hinge alignment itself a self-consistent retained-history configuration (own root-budget, causal-margin, pump balance)? Same formation-history burden, now a concrete nested-shell target. Fail-closed throughout: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema.

Verification: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-cross-hit-hinge-coexistence-diagnostic.test.js` -> 5 pass, 0 fail.


## 2026-07-08 - Super-field inner-binary self-consistency: the two routes are one crux (item 11)

Executing the prior closure goal: is the inner binary at $\beta_{\mathrm{in}}\gtrsim1$ (required by the cross-hit absorber) itself a self-consistent retained-history configuration, or unsustainable? New owner script [super-field-inner-binary-consistency-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/super-field-inner-binary-consistency-diagnostic.mjs) (tests [braid-ideal-super-field-inner-binary-consistency-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-super-field-inner-binary-consistency-diagnostic.test.js), 5 passing) and [spec Section 10](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#10-self-consistency-of-the-super-field-inner-binary-the-two-routes-are-one-crux-2026-07-08).

- Result A (no inner absorber): a click needs a super-field source ($|A_{ij}|\le|\mathbf v_j|=\beta_j c_f$). The outer shell is sub-field ($\max A_{\mathrm{out}\to\mathrm{in}}=0.43<c_f$, cannot straddle); same-shell partners share the inner frequency ($A_{ij}$ constant to $2.4\times10^{-15}$). So the inner binary's only possible cross-hit absorber is a strictly faster distinct third shell.
- Result B (regress diverges): shell $k$ needs a faster super-field shell $k{+}1$; the tower is strictly increasing and bounded below by 1, so it never terminates on a cross-hit. The click relocates the pump inward rather than dissipating it.
- Result C (terminal reduction): the innermost shell has only breathing ($\le27\%$), sea ($\le10\%$), and the self-hit; only the self-hit can reach the pump, but its magnitude is the $d_0$ coincidence-stratum question ($\approx50\times$ pump, not chart-clean), and above $\beta=1$ the partner pump is itself uncertified (the interval certificate bails for $\beta\ge1$). Terminal balance couples the uncertified super-field pump and the $d_0$-dependent self-hit.
- Result D (self-hit is super-field): $F(\Delta)=2\rho\sin(\beta\Delta/2\rho)-c_f\Delta$, $F'(0)=\beta-1$: no self-hit root below field speed, born at $\beta=1$, finite count (order 1 through $\beta\sim3$). Self-hit brake and cross-hit inner binary share the super-field regime.

Verdict `super_field_inner_binary_self_consistency_reduces_to_d0_self_hit_balance_cross_hit_and_self_hit_routes_are_one_crux`: the cross-hit route does not close negatively but unifies with the self-hit route - both terminal closures reduce to the $d_0$-set self-hit tangential balance on a super-field shell. This removes the illusion that cross-hit and self-hit were independent hedges; the retention hunt's two surviving candidates are one crux. Fail-closed throughout: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema.

Next closure goal: compute the super-field tangential force balance on a single rigid shell for $\beta\gtrsim1$ - the uncertified partner-wake pump plus the $d_0$-regularized self-hit brake - and determine whether it has a zero at an admissible $\beta^*$ (self-balancing terminal shell, retention closes affirmatively) or stays net anti-damping (no terminal shell, forcing Noether-sea/field dissipation as the only remaining sink).

Verification: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-super-field-inner-binary-consistency-diagnostic.test.js` -> 5 pass, 0 fail.


## 2026-07-08 - Super-field tangential balance: the field-speed pin (item 11) + normal-convention audit

Operator asked to FIRST verify all cross-hit math handles source-normal and receiver-normal correctly, then compute the super-field balance.

Audit (against production `receiverNormalFields` in [AbsoluteHistoryRootRuntime.mjs](../../../src/solver/app/AbsoluteHistoryRootRuntime.mjs)): canonical convention is $\hat{\mathbf r}=(\mathbf x_{\mathrm{rec}}-\mathbf x_{\mathrm{src}})/|\cdots|$ (source$\to$receiver), $D_s=c_f-\mathbf v_{\mathrm{src}}\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}$ (jacobian/denominator), $D_T=c_f-\mathbf v_{\mathrm{rec}}\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}$ (numerator, same $\hat{\mathbf r}$), $m=D_T/D_s$ (signed), branchWeight $=|m|$. The click-rate/coexistence/consistency diagnostics use the source-normal correctly (their alignment scalar $=c_f-D_s$ to $2\times10^{-16}$), but they summed impulse MAGNITUDE only, so the absorptive/ejective sign $\operatorname{sign}(D_T)$ was not evaluated there - fine for counts/availability, but the balance needs signed $m$ and must not assume $D_s>0$ (super-field). Existing fold/self-hit scripts and the interval certificate use the same convention (certificate uses unsigned $|D_T|/D_s$ and requires $D_s>0$, valid only sub-field).

New owner script [super-field-tangential-balance-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/super-field-tangential-balance-diagnostic.mjs) (tests [braid-ideal-super-field-tangential-balance-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-super-field-tangential-balance-diagnostic.test.js), 5 passing) and [spec Section 11](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#11-super-field-tangential-balance-the-field-speed-pin-2026-07-08):

- Validation: the signed-normal point evaluator reproduces the certified band $2.881\beta\le\Phi_{\mathrm{tan}}\le2.925\beta$ for $\beta\in[0.1,0.985]$ (measured $\Phi/\beta\in[2.888,2.919]$, all in-band); signed=unsigned sub-field ($D_T>0$).
- Result 1: extended across $\beta=1$, the partner pump stays anti-damping and grows ($\Phi_{\mathrm{tan}}=2.87,2.92,2.98,3.08,3.24,3.58,4.28$ at $\beta=0.985..1.4$), single-root, non-caustic ($D_s>0$). The pump does NOT switch off super-field - the Section 10 "uncertified pump might vanish" escape is closed.
- Result 2 (inputs, provenance recorded): self-hit brake absorptive ($m<0$), onsets at $\beta=1$, $\sim50\times$ pump at declared $d_0$ (exact size $d_0$-gated).
- Result 3: no static super-field $\beta^*$ (pump only grows); switching structure gives a dynamic field-speed pin - above $\beta=1$ net braking pushes down, below $\beta=1$ the pump pushes up, so $\beta\approx1$ is a self-limiting attractor.

Verdict `partner_pump_persists_super_field_no_static_beta_star_switching_self_hit_yields_field_speed_pin_gated_on_d0_sea_field_dissipation_not_required`: the retention hunt closes affirmatively-conditionally - a terminal shell exists (the field-speed-pinned inner binary) and does NOT require Noether-sea/field dissipation; gated on $d_0$ for exact pin stability (declared $\sim50\times$ suffices). Claim: derivation+measurement for the validated super-field pump; hypothesis for the pin as a proven attractor (trajectory needs the retained-history solver). Fail-closed throughout: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema.

Next closure goal: confirm the field-speed pin as an actual attractor on the native retained-history solver (a two-shell row with the inner binary released near $\beta=1$), and invert the pin balance $\Phi_{\mathrm{partner}}(1)+\Phi_{\mathrm{self}}(1;d_0)=0$ to DERIVE $d_0$ as the coincidence stratum that makes the field-speed edge marginally stable - turning the open $d_0$ value (particle-masses.md) into a fixed point of the retention balance rather than a free operator input.

Verification: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-super-field-tangential-balance-diagnostic.test.js` -> 5 pass, 0 fail.


## 2026-07-08 - Field-speed pin: attractor confirmed (reduced), d0 inversion is a bound (item 11)

Executing the prior closure goal: confirm the field-speed pin as an attractor and invert the balance for d0. New owner script [field-speed-pin-attractor-diagnostic.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/field-speed-pin-attractor-diagnostic.mjs) (tests [braid-ideal-field-speed-pin-attractor-diagnostic.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-field-speed-pin-attractor-diagnostic.test.js), 6 passing) and [spec Section 12](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#12-field-speed-pin-attractor-confirmation-and-d0-inversion-2026-07-08). Reduced reference integrator (validated signed-normal partner pump + switching self-hit brake); explicitly NOT the native solver - native retained-history confirmation stays gated.

Modeling correction found during the run: the self-hit brake is a MULTIPLE of the LOCAL pump, not a fixed magnitude. So $\Phi_{\mathrm{net}}(\beta)=\Phi_{\mathrm{partner}}(\beta)[1-\varrho\,\Theta(\beta-1)]$ and, since the partner pump grows super-field, the pin threshold is the ratio $\varrho>1$ (not brake=pump at a single beta).

- Result 1 (attractor): for $\varrho>1$ the field-speed edge $\beta=1$ is a two-sided attractor - releases $\{0.9,0.95,1.05,1.1\}$ all converge to $\beta\approx1$ (to $1.00$ at $\varrho=1.5$, to $\approx0.98$ at the declared $\varrho\approx50$). $\varrho=1$ marginal (attracts from below, neutral above); $\varrho=0.5$ super-field runaway (no pin).
- Result 2 (d0 inversion): the marginal condition $\varrho=1$ against the measured self-hit-brake-vs-stratum curve (absorbed fraction $0.031/0.115/0.371/2.69/9.67$ at $\rho_c=0.2/0.1/0.05/0.01/0.001$) lands at $\rho_c^*\approx2.2\times10^{-2}$. The pin holds for $d_0\le\rho_c^*$ - an INEQUALITY, not a unique fixed point, because over-braking is also stable. The declared $d_0=R_{\mathrm{MCB}}\sim\kappa\epsilon^2/c_f^2$ ($\varrho\approx50$) sits well inside the stable range and gives a firm, over-damped pin.

Honest correction to the closure goal: inverting the balance does NOT uniquely derive $d_0$; it BOUNDS it ($d_0\le\rho_c^*\approx0.022$, equivalently the self-hit brake must exceed the partner pump above field speed). The unique $d_0$ still comes from $R_{\mathrm{MCB}}$ physics, now bounded above by the retention balance. Verdict `field_speed_edge_two_sided_attractor_for_ratio_above_one_balance_bounds_d0_by_marginal_stratum_declared_d0_over_damped_inside_range`. Claim: derivation+measurement (reference) for the attractor and marginal stratum; hypothesis for the native-solver pin (gated) and for $\varrho(\beta)$ staying $>1$ as $\beta$ grows (its $\beta$-dependence unmeasured). Fail-closed throughout: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema.

Next closure goal: (a) native retained-history confirmation of the pin (gated two-shell row released near $\beta=1$); (b) measure $\varrho(\beta)$ above field speed - if it stays $>1$ the pin is unconditional in the super-field range, else there is an upper escape; (c) reconcile $\rho_c^*\approx0.022$ against $R_{\mathrm{MCB}}\sim\kappa\epsilon^2/c_f^2$ (particle-masses.md).

Verification: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-field-speed-pin-attractor-diagnostic.test.js` -> 6 pass, 0 fail.


## 2026-07-08 - Signed re-check of the cross-hit coexistence absorption (item 11)

Executing the audit-flagged proof re-check. Section 9 summed cross-hit click MAGNITUDES ("~3x surplus" absorption) without evaluating the per-click receiver-normal sign $m=D_T/D_s$. Redone with the signed convention (validated in Section 11). New owner script [cross-hit-signed-absorption-recheck.mjs](../../../scripts/braid-ideal/cross-hit-signed-absorption-recheck.mjs) (tests [braid-ideal-cross-hit-signed-absorption-recheck.test.js](../../../tests/braid-ideal-cross-hit-signed-absorption-recheck.test.js), 3 passing) and [spec Section 13](fold-crossing-chart-spec.md#13-signed-re-check-of-the-cross-hit-coexistence-absorption-2026-07-08).

- Result 1 (sign = polarity, robust): for a fixed source->receiver polarity product the tangential transfer has a definite sign (net = +/- |sum|, no per-pair cancellation). Like-polarity source -> net absorptive; opposite-polarity -> net ejective. Regulator-robust sign.
- Result 2 (neutral binary self-cancels): the physical inner binary is neutral (one +, one -), so its members deliver opposite-sign transfers to each outer receiver that substantially cancel (cancellation grows with fold-pole resolution, ~25% coarse to >=90% fine). - Caveat: the instantaneous-proxy magnitudes (both net and |sum|) are not converged - they grow as the $D_s\to0$ fold pole is resolved. A converged number needs the causal root-sum / density-of-states, not the instantaneous reception-time integral.

Verdict: the Section 9 "~3x surplus" overstated the case (magnitude sum + regulator dependence). The coexistence NECESSARY conditions (Section 8/9: enough clicks + straddling) are sign-independent and stand; the SUFFICIENCY (neutral inner binary net-absorbs the pump via cross-hits) is NOT established. This does NOT affect the field-speed pin (Sections 11-12), which rests on the self-hit/partner-pump balance, and it reinforces Section 10 (the self-hit pin is the load-bearing terminal absorber; the cross-hit is not an independent clean absorber). A dated correction pointer was added to Section 9. Fail-closed throughout: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`, no accepted seed-path certificate, no new validator or schema.

Only the $s=0.5$, $T=6$ run clears one memory depth, and barely: the late-time delay grows to $4.9164908$, so six time units buy $\approx1.2$ late-time delays, on one seed.

The prediction's mechanism is refuted (the self-hit pumps) but no evolution bears on whether the rail attracts, and "two-sided" is vacuous while no through-rail continuation exists. The singular-boundary result is held at **derivation-grade on the circular family**; the caustic $D_s^{\rm self}\to1-s\to0$ is expected generic and unproven off the manifold.
- Dispatch packet corrections: the self hinge is $\delta=2s\lvert\sin(\delta/2)\rvert$ (the unsigned form drops every root with $\delta>2\pi$, real from $s\approx4.61$); the primary-root force form is valid only for $s\lesssim2.99$; the $s=10$ figures are $-1.506$ against $+4.827$, not $-2.95$ against $+12.14$; $F_r(3)=+0.849$, not $+0.312$; $\varrho(10)=0.31$, not $0.24$. Conclusions unaffected, magnitudes above $s\approx3$ wrong. The spindle-braid $\varrho$ contradiction verdict is unchanged — still on the brake-loses side.

- **Advancement — closed-form root-count certificate, both channels.** A root pair is born where $y=\delta$ goes tangent to $y=2s\lvert f(\delta/2)\rvert$; both tangencies sit at radius $2\sqrt{s^2-1}$. Partner: $\sqrt{s^2-1}+\arcsin(1/s)=\pi m$. Self: $\sqrt{s^2-1}-\arccos(1/s)=\pi m$. They interleave because $\arcsin(1/s)+\arccos(1/s)=\pi/2$. This discharges the dispatch's root-completeness gate by formula at every $s$ instead of per row. Confirmed by two independent routes — closed form vs bracket scan — agreeing on all six thresholds. The **self family was already promoted** at `content/markdown/aaa/dynamics/binary-dynamics.md:868`; the dispatch restated that file's principal branch as the whole equation and dropped the winding family 500 lines below it, so the campaign's $s=3$/$s=5$ multi-root surprises were a regression against the corpus, not discoveries. The partner family is the new companion and is a promotion candidate alongside it.
- Refuted-prediction row: the successor adjudication briefing carried the unsigned self hinge and the single-root $s=10$ figures as "verified on the independent 90-digit oracle." Neither was. A handoff summary promoted its source's claim level by paraphrase; caught by measuring, as every prior instance of this role's errors has been.
